# Full keyword mapping report (all indexable URLs)

Generated from the refreshed Semrush pool (KD 0-32, volume 20+, 6810 keywords). Nothing is committed and no site content was changed. Complete machine-readable version: `docs/seo/full-keyword-report.csv` (one row per URL, 3007 rows, including every individual product).

Key: [intent volume/KD]. Intent letters: I informational, C commercial, N navigational, T transactional.

## 1. Totals

| Type | URLs | With primary | of which fallback (shared parent-category primary) | Primary but too few secondaries | No primary | Fully compliant (primary + secondary count + intent rules) |

Utility/legal pages (About, Contact, FAQ, Help, Refunds, Terms, Privacy, California privacy, PACT Act, FDA disclaimer) are counted under "no primary" because the recommendation is no keyword target; this still needs your confirmation.

**Name-based primaries (the fallback column):** where no Semrush keyword at KD 0-32 fits a page, its primary is the page's own name (brand name, collection name, product name or post title), marked "N (name-based)" with volume and KD "n/a" because no Semrush data exists for it. Secondaries on these pages come from the parent category (transactional or commercial only). Each name-based primary is unique to its page, so these pages no longer share a primary with their category page. They should be re-checked in Semrush later and upgraded to a real keyword if one appears.

Non-indexable URLs (cart, checkout, account, wishlist, search, order, thank-you, admin) are excluded.

## 2. How keywords are placed (rules applied to every row)

| Where | Primary keyphrase | Secondary keywords |
|---|---|---|
| H1 (one per page) | yes, mandatory | no |
| SEO title | yes, near the start, plus 1-2 power words (Buy, Shop, Best, Order, Get, Official...) | no |
| Meta description | yes | yes (first 2 secondaries) |
| URL / slug | yes (see "primary in slug" column; existing slugs are not changed without approval) | no |
| First paragraph / first 10% of copy | yes | no |
| Body copy | yes, about 2% density | yes, about 3% combined density |
| H2/H3 | in some, never skipping levels | first 5 secondaries in H2/H3 (many written as questions) |
| Image alt text and file names | yes | yes (alt only) |
| Tags | yes | yes |
| FAQ (mandatory on every page) | yes | secondaries 3-5 |

Minimum copy length: blog 1,500 words; pages 600; products 400; categories, subcategories and brand pages 300. Each blog post also links 4 product primaries as secondary keywords with the product link embedded (not yet mapped, see section 8).

Secondary counts: products, categories, subcategories, brands 5-10 (T/C only); pages and blog 10-15 (T and C, max 2 navigational, max 2 informational).

## 6. Shared primaries (cannibalization risk)

## 8. Not yet mapped (outside the keyword pool)

- Blog posts need 4 product primaries embedded as links in each post (rule 3). The product-primary list exists per family above; the 4 links per post are chosen when each post is written.
- Tags, image alt text and image file names are assigned at content time from the placement columns in the CSV.
- Pagination pages (/page/2 etc.) are canonicalised/noindexed per the technical audit and inherit the first page's keywords; they are not mapped separately.
